M10-R v2.23 IMG-SYSTEM DNG WRITER / ASN PROVENANCE
Scope: 092_IMG-System.bin only. No cross-section raw 0xC628 scan.
Goal: identify a real DNG tag table/writer neighborhood and test whether AsShotNeutral is sourced from the same per-frame WB/ASN state frozen in v1.45.
